# Event 029 Riches Found decision and mission layer.
#
# This file owns state-targeted actions, mission lifecycles, route-aware AI willingness, concrete
# resource costs, and local outcome flags. The companion trigger/effect files provide the mine
# marker, selected-mine gate, controller reconciliation, and bounded presentation refresh contract.
# No Event 018, biological-outbreak, world-end, or scripted-GUI code is used here.
# The state owner sets riches_found_mine_state and the phase/evolution flags consumed below.

# Decision costs, duration bands, thresholds, and AI willingness factors are shared through
# common/script_constants/029_riches_found_constants.txt.

# ai_hint_pp_cost requires static values; these aliases mirror the pp_* keys
# in riches_found_decision_cost (common/script_constants/029_riches_found_constants.txt).
@RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT = 10
@RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD = 25
@RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY = 40

# Decision modifiers require static values; these mirror civ_standard and
# civ_heavy in riches_found_decision_cost.
@RICHES_FOUND_CIVILIAN_FACTORY_STANDARD = 2
@RICHES_FOUND_CIVILIAN_FACTORY_HEAVY = 3

# AI scenario coverage is kept beside the willingness blocks: RF_POLICY_01_STABLE_DEMOCRACY,
# RF_POLICY_02_AUTHORITARIAN_MAJOR_AT_WAR, RF_POLICY_03_POOR_MINOR, RF_POLICY_04_COLONIAL_OCCUPIER,
# RF_DEV_01_INLAND_DEEP_REEF, RF_DEV_02_ISLAND_GEMSTONE, RF_DEV_03_COLLAPSE_WARNING,
# RF_REV_01_HIGH_LEGITIMACY_DEMOCRACY, RF_REV_02_WARTIME_FINANCIAL_CRISIS, RF_REV_03_LOW_LEGITIMACY_NON_CORE,
# RF_FOREIGN_01_NEAR_ALLIED_MAJOR, RF_FOREIGN_02_DISTANT_NAVAL_POWER, RF_FOREIGN_03_HOSTILE_RIVAL,
# RF_FOREIGN_04_EXISTING_EXCLUSIVE_CONCESSION, RF_SEC_01_CIVILIAN_CRIME, RF_SEC_02_ARMED_RAID_WARTIME,
# RF_SEC_03_WORKER_CLAIM_WAR, RF_SEC_04_PRIVATE_ENCLAVE, RF_EVO1_01_MANAGED_MINE,
# RF_EVO1_02_CAPTURED_CONCESSION, RF_EVO1_03_MULTIPLE_MINES, RF_EVO2_01_HIGH_PRESSURE_GEMSTONE,
# RF_EVO2_02_SHARED_REVENUE_CONTROLLED_ACCESS, RF_EVO2_03_CLOSED_MINE, RF_EVO3_01_AGGRESSIVE_DEEP_MINE,
# RF_EVO3_02_PARTIALLY_SEALED, RF_EVO3_03_GOLD_DISEASE_MASS_VIOLENCE, RF_ACCOUNT_01_STABLE_STATE,
# RF_ACCOUNT_02_DESPERATE_WAR_STATE, RF_ACCOUNT_03_LOW_STABILITY_HIGH_DEPENDENCE,
# RF_DEMON_01_STABLE_DEMOCRACY_PEACE, RF_DEMON_02_COLLAPSING_AUTHORITARIAN_WAR, and
# RF_DEMON_03_EVACUATION_COMPLETE. These are willingness scenarios, never player-facing probabilities.

riches_found_mine_management = {

decision_riches_found_survey = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_survey
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_survey = yes }
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_survey = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_survey
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_survey_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_mine_development value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.development_ready compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}
}

decision_riches_found_claim_register = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_claim_register
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_claim_register = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered }
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_claim_register = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_claim_register
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_claim_register_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.secure_rush }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.secure_rush_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_registered
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_7 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_6 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_concession = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
	}
}
decision_riches_found_claim_freeze = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_claim_freeze
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_claim_freeze = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_claim_freeze = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_claim_freeze
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.emergency
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_claim_freeze_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_light_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.secure_rush }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.secure_rush_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_frozen
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_15 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_light
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_militarized = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = communism }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = fascism }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
	}
	}

decision_riches_found_recognize_local_claims = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_claim_register
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_present
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_recognized }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_recognize_local_claims = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_present
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_recognized }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_present
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_recognized }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_recognize_local_claims = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_recognize_local_claims
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_recognize_local_claims_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.secure_rush }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.secure_rush_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_local_claims_recognized
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_15 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_3 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_revenue_legitimacy value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.legitimacy_floor compare = less_than } } }
	}
}

decision_riches_found_reserve_deposit = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_claim_freeze
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_state_reserve }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_reserve_deposit = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_state_reserve }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_survey_complete
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_state_reserve }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_reserve_deposit = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_secure_rush_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_reserve_deposit
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_reserve_deposit_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.secure_rush }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.secure_rush_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_state_reserve
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_4 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_concession = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = communism }
	}
}

			has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_unresolved

		}
	}
	available = {

		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims
			has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_unresolved
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_break_claim_war_subject }
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {

			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_claims

		}

	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_claims_court_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.break_claim_war }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.break_claim_war_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
				set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_resolved
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_unresolved
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_armed_claimants
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_2 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = greater_than } } }
	}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_camp_administration
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
						has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
						NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_unresolved }
					}
					set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_resolved
				}
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_mine_profile = constant:riches_found_profile.alluvial } } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }

			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }

				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.train_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_concession = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_mine_development value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.development_ready compare = less_than } } }
	}

			has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route_valid

		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_development
			has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route_valid
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
		}

		riches_found_country_can_start_open_railhead_mission = yes

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.train_standard_negative }

			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.open_railhead_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route
				riches_found_build_rail_spur = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.train_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }

						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.never_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_island_mine } }

		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_shafts_reinforced
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_collapse_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
				riches_found_record_achievement_repair_completed = yes
				riches_found_record_achievement_assistance = yes
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_mine_profile = constant:riches_found_profile.deep_vein } } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged } }
	}

		}
		else = {

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy }

			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_development
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_development_fund }

			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_development

			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_development has_state_flag = riches_found_deep_excavation_authorized }

	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_drive_deeper_tt
		FROM = { set_state_flag = riches_found_deep_project_active set_variable = { riches_found_deep_project_controller = ROOT } }
		riches_found_refresh_controller_aggregate = yes

		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative

		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative

		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
					has_state_flag = riches_found_deep_project_active
					check_variable = { var = riches_found_deep_project_controller value = ROOT compare = equals }
					NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
					NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_collapsed }
				}

					clr_state_flag = riches_found_deep_project_active
					clear_variable = riches_found_deep_project_controller
				}
			}
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy }
		}
	}

		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }

		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_dividend = yes }
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_revenue
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_dividend_paid }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_revenue
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_dividend_paid }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_dividend = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_dividend
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_dividend_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_dividend_paid
				riches_found_record_revenue_settlement = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_public_settlement_progress }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_public_project_complete
					set_state_flag = riches_found_public_revenue_share_paid
				}
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_4 }
				riches_found_record_achievement_settlement = yes
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_revenue_legitimacy value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.legitimacy_floor compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_revenue_legitimacy value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_high compare = greater_than } } }
	}

		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_stabilization_fund = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_public_settlement_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_revenue
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_stabilization_fund }
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_revenue

	}

		riches_found_can_afford_stabilization_fund = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_public_settlement_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_stabilization_fund
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_stabilization_fund_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.public_settlement }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.public_settlement_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_stabilization_fund
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_5 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }

			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_foreign

		}

	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_offtake
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_offtake_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.convoy_standard_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = { riches_found_contract_is_active_and_valid = yes }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_offtake_agreement
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_foreign_claim_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
					riches_found_extend_limited_contract = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_state_flag = riches_found_offtake_agreement
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_foreign_claim_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
					set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_contract_type = constant:riches_found_contract_type.offtake }
					riches_found_prepare_foreign_partner = yes
					if = { limit = { has_event_target = riches_found_contract_partner_target } riches_found_begin_contract = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { riches_found_report_kind = constant:riches_found_report.concession }
				riches_found_queue_report = yes
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.convoy_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_concession = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_allied_target_available } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_war = yes }
	}

		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_nationalized
				set_state_flag = riches_found_compensation_settlement_paid
				set_state_flag = riches_found_compensation_claim_resolved
				set_variable = { riches_found_compensation_settlement_date = global.date }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = riches_found_contract_partner }
					set_variable = { riches_found_compensated_former_partner = riches_found_contract_partner }
				}
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_foreign_claim_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				riches_found_compensate_contract_partner = yes
				riches_found_end_contract = yes
				set_variable = { riches_found_contract_stage = constant:riches_found_contract_stage.nationalized }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_concession = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}

					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {

				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_private_security_autonomy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_concession = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_private_enclave } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor has_government = democratic }

	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_security
			has_state_flag = riches_found_raid_crisis
			riches_found_state_has_supplied_force = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_army_cordon }
		}
	}

		riches_found_can_afford_army_cordon = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_hold_mine_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_army_cordon
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_army_cordon_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_heavy_negative }
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative

			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.hold_mine }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.hold_mine_during_war_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_army_cordon
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_15 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_3 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {

			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_heavy }
			add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_militarized = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_raid_crisis } }

		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_security
			has_state_flag = riches_found_armed_claimants
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_break_claim_war_subject }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_truce = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_break_claim_war_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_truce
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_truce_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.break_claim_war }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.break_claim_war_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_mine_truce
				set_state_flag = riches_found_claims_resolved
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_unresolved
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_armed_claimants
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_worker_claim_war } }
	}
}

		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_emergency
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_deep_mine has_state_flag = riches_found_collapse_warning has_state_flag = riches_found_gold_disease_active has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_signs }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_depths_sealed }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_emergency
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_deep_mine has_state_flag = riches_found_collapse_warning has_state_flag = riches_found_gold_disease_active has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_signs }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_depths_sealed }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_seal_depths = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_seal_depths
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_seal_depths_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_depths_sealed
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_18 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_deep_excavation = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_mine_profile = constant:riches_found_profile.deep_vein } } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_extraction_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { OR = { check_variable = { riches_found_demons_next_step = constant:riches_found_containment_step.demons_sealing } check_variable = { riches_found_demons_next_step = constant:riches_found_containment_step.demons_integrity } } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_collapse_warning } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}
}

decision_riches_found_repair = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_repair
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged
			NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_repair = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_restore_shaft_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged
			NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject }
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged
			NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_repair = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_restore_shaft_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_repair
	modifier = { civilian_factory_use = @RICHES_FOUND_CIVILIAN_FACTORY_STANDARD }
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.long_project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_repair_tt
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.restore_shaft }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.restore_shaft_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The repair crews deliver only to a mine this country still holds with
		# its Restore the Shaft mission running; otherwise the payment returns.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_repair_started
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
			add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_mine_development value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.development_ready compare = greater_than } } }
	}
}

decision_riches_found_reopen_mine = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_reopen_mine
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			riches_found_mine_closure_can_reopen = yes
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_reopen_mine = yes }
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			hidden_trigger = { riches_found_mine_closure_can_reopen = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = decision_riches_found_reopen_mine_safe_tt
				riches_found_mine_reopening_is_safe = yes
			}
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			riches_found_mine_closure_can_reopen = yes
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_reopen_mine = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_reopen_mine
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_duration.recovery_review_days
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_reopen_mine_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
		FROM = { riches_found_begin_reopening = yes }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The inspection ends with a reopening only while this country still
		# holds the closed mine and it is still safe; otherwise the closure
		# stays and the payment returns.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
					has_state_flag = riches_found_reopening_in_progress
					riches_found_mine_reopening_is_safe = yes
					NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged }
				}
			}
			FROM = { riches_found_finish_reopening = yes }
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { riches_found_cancel_reopening = yes }
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_concession = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_mine_development value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.development_ready compare = greater_than } } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_gold_disease_active } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_beneath_mine_active } }
	}
}

			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_occupation
			NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
			riches_found_occupation_mine_can_reopen = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_reopened }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_occupation
			NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
			riches_found_occupation_mine_can_reopen = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_reopened }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_occupation_reopen = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_occupation_reopen
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_occupation_reopen_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_standard_negative }
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_temporary_closure
						NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_containment_pending }
					}
					riches_found_begin_recovery = yes
					riches_found_complete_recovery = yes
				}
				set_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_reopened
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				riches_found_record_achievement_transfer_settlement = yes
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard
			add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_state } }
	}
}
