		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_occupation_strip_equipment_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_light_negative
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_stripped
				set_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_stripped_history
				set_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_15 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_20 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_5 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_light
			add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_retreat_imminent } }
	}
}

	visible = {

			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_occupation
			NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
			riches_found_occupation_mine_can_be_denied = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_denial }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_occupation_deny_mine = yes }
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_occupation
			NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }

		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_phase_occupation
			NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
			riches_found_occupation_mine_can_be_denied = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_denial }
		}
	}

	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_occupation_deny_mine
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_occupation_deny_mine_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_standard_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_denial
				set_state_flag = riches_found_occupation_denial_history
				set_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_25 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_20 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
				if = {
					limit = { riches_found_mine_can_begin_temporary_closure = yes }
					riches_found_begin_temporary_closure = yes
				}
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_militarized = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_retreat_imminent } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor has_government = democratic }
	}
}

	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_stabilization_fund
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_resource_curse_active
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_curse_diversification_started }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_curse_stabilization_diversification = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_public_settlement_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_resource_curse_active
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_curse_diversification_started }
		}
		ROOT = { riches_found_country_has_free_mission_slot = yes }
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_resource_curse_active
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_curse_diversification_started }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_curse_stabilization_diversification = yes
		riches_found_country_can_start_public_settlement_mission = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_curse_stabilization_diversification
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.long_project

	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_curse_stabilization_diversification_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_kind = constant:riches_found_mission_kind.public_settlement }
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_input_mission_duration_days = constant:riches_found_duration.public_settlement_days }
			riches_found_start_requested_mission = yes
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# The staged result lands only while this country still holds the
		# registered, nonterminal mine; otherwise every cost is returned.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_curse_diversification_started
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_curse_next_step = constant:riches_found_containment_step.curse_fund } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}

	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_LIGHT
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_curse_tolerate_corruption_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {

				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_15 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_20 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_curse_corruption_paid } } }
				add_political_power = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12
				FROM = { set_state_flag = riches_found_curse_corruption_paid }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_light
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_curse_containment value = constant:riches_found_value.zero compare = greater_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = fascism }
	}

		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_gold_military_control_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_heavy_negative }
		add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard_negative

				}
			}

				clr_state_flag = riches_found_gold_sick_gang_control
				set_state_flag = riches_found_confiscated_material
				riches_found_move_gold_through_rotation = yes

		}

		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_militarized = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_gold_next_step = constant:riches_found_containment_step.gold_material } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = fascism }

		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = riches_found_confiscated_material_destroyed
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_confiscated_material
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_beneath_mine_active }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_containment.destroy_material_supernatural_add }
				}
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_gold_disease_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_18 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_6 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_heavy }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.motorized_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_gold_next_step = constant:riches_found_containment_step.gold_material } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_gold_disease_active } }
	}
}

decision_riches_found_demons_evacuate_district = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_evacuation
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_beneath_mine_active
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_severe_collapse has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_crisis }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_district_evacuated }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_pending }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_demons_evacuate_district = yes }
		riches_found_country_can_start_evacuate_workforce_mission = yes
		FROM = {
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route has_state_flag = riches_found_port_route }
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_beneath_mine_active
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_severe_collapse has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_crisis }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_district_evacuated }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_pending }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_beneath_mine_active
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_severe_collapse has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_crisis }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_district_evacuated }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_pending }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_demons_evacuate_district = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_demons_evacuate_district
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_duration.evacuation_preparation_days
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_demons_evacuate_district_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_emergency_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.train_standard_negative }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.convoy_heavy_negative }
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
			set_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_pending
			set_variable = { riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_date = global.date }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:riches_found_duration.evacuation_preparation_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_date = chaosx_date_offset }
		}
	}
	remove_effect = {
		# This decision alone resolves the preparation. It starts the
		# evacuation while the payer still holds a mine that can start it;
		# otherwise the authorization is withdrawn and every cost returns.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
					riches_found_demons_evacuation_can_start = yes
				}
			}
			FROM = { riches_found_start_demons_evacuation = yes }
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { riches_found_cancel_demons_evacuation_preparation = yes }
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_emergency
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.train_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.convoy_heavy }
		}
		FROM = { riches_found_refresh_state_achievement_timers = yes }
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_demons_next_step = constant:riches_found_containment_step.demons_evacuation } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_crisis } }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}
}

			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_beneath_mine_active
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_occult_assistance }
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_beneath_mine_active
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_occult_assistance }
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_demons_occult_assistance = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_demons_occult_assistance
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_demons_occult_assistance_tt
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light_negative
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					riches_found_mine_still_valid_for_payer = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				set_temp_variable = { riches_found_containment_days = constant:riches_found_containment.assistance_days }
				set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_demons_occult_assistance days = riches_found_containment_days }
				set_state_flag = riches_found_demons_occult_assistance_history
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_20 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_4 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_illicit_capture = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { check_variable = { riches_found_demons_next_step = constant:riches_found_containment_step.demons_assistance } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = neutrality }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_occult_network_available } }
	}

decision_riches_found_account_close = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_close_account
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
				has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_closed }
			riches_found_account_resolution_can_start = yes
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_account_close = yes }
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
				has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_closed }
			riches_found_account_resolution_can_start = yes
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
			has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_closed }
			riches_found_account_resolution_can_start = yes
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_account_close = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_account_close
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_STANDARD
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.project
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_account_close_tt
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_account_input_resolution_choice = constant:riches_found_account_resolution_choice.close }
			riches_found_begin_account_resolution_receipt = yes
		}
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard_negative
		add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
					NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
					has_state_flag = riches_found_account_resolution_pending
					check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_resolution_controller value = ROOT compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_resolution_choice value = constant:riches_found_account_resolution_choice.close compare = equals }
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				riches_found_begin_account_containment = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_30 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_25 }
				riches_found_clamp_mine_values = yes
				riches_found_record_achievement_account_closure = yes
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			FROM = {
				set_temp_variable = { riches_found_account_expected_resolution_choice = constant:riches_found_account_resolution_choice.close }
				riches_found_clear_owned_account_resolution_receipt = yes
			}
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_standard
			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_breach_imminent } }
	}
}

	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_bargain
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
	visible = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
				has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_terms_bound }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_closed }
			riches_found_account_resolution_can_start = yes
			riches_found_account_binding_is_valid = yes
		}
	}
	available = {
		hidden_trigger = { riches_found_can_afford_account_bind_terms = yes }
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
				has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_terms_bound }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_closed }
			riches_found_account_resolution_can_start = yes
			riches_found_account_binding_is_valid = yes
		}
	}
	target_root_trigger = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			riches_found_state_is_active_selected_or_only_mine = yes
				has_state_flag = riches_found_bottomless_account
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_terms_bound }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_closed }
			riches_found_account_resolution_can_start = yes
			riches_found_account_binding_is_valid = yes
		}
	}
	custom_cost_trigger = {
		riches_found_can_afford_account_bind_terms = yes
	}
	custom_cost_text = riches_found_cost_account_bind_terms
	ai_hint_pp_cost = @RICHES_FOUND_AI_HINT_PP_HEAVY
	days_remove = constant:riches_found_decision_duration.admin
	fire_only_once = no
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_account_bind_terms_tt
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_account_input_resolution_choice = constant:riches_found_account_resolution_choice.bind }
			riches_found_begin_account_resolution_receipt = yes
		}
		add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy_negative
		add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard_negative
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard_negative }
	}
	remove_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
					NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
					has_state_flag = riches_found_account_resolution_pending
					check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_resolution_controller value = ROOT compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_resolution_choice value = constant:riches_found_account_resolution_choice.bind compare = equals }
					riches_found_account_binding_is_valid = yes
				}
			}

			FROM = {
				riches_found_bind_account_terms = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			FROM = {
				set_temp_variable = { riches_found_account_expected_resolution_choice = constant:riches_found_account_resolution_choice.bind }
				riches_found_clear_owned_account_resolution_receipt = yes
			}
			add_political_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.pp_heavy
			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_standard }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_state = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_account_active } }
	}
}

			}

			add_manpower = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.manpower_light
			add_fuel = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.fuel_standard

					check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_payment_controller value = ROOT compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_payment_choice value = constant:riches_found_account_payment_choice.predatory compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_pending_obligation value = riches_found_account_next_obligation compare = equals }
					riches_found_account_predatory_terms_are_valid = yes
				}

			FROM = {

				set_temp_variable = { riches_found_account_payment_family = riches_found_account_pending_obligation }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_account_payment_family value = constant:riches_found_account_obligation.industrial_sacrifice compare = equals } }
					subtract_from_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_account_terms.industrial_development_cost }
				}
				else = { riches_found_record_predatory_prosperity_harm = yes }
				riches_found_fulfil_account_obligation = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_15 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_20 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_20 }
			}
			riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
			riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes

		}
		else = {
			FROM = {
				set_temp_variable = { riches_found_account_expected_payment_choice = constant:riches_found_account_payment_choice.predatory }
				riches_found_clear_owned_account_payment_receipt = yes
			}
			add_command_power = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.command_standard
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.infantry_standard }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:riches_found_decision_cost.support_light }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_favored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_predatory = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.policy_disfavored_factor FROM = { riches_found_policy_profile_public = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor is_major = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_multiple_mine_priority } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.low_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_war = yes }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = fascism }
						modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.never_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_supernatural_pressure value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.pressure_severe compare = less_than } } }
	}
}

# Multiple mines are managed through one selected state. The selector is
# intentionally in the same ordinary category so it cannot create a second
# category or a parallel scripted-GUI surface.
decision_riches_found_select_mine = {
	icon = GFX_decision_riches_found_survey
	state_target = any_controlled_state
	on_map_mode = map_only
	visible = {
		is_ai = no
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
			NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
		}
		OR = { riches_found_country_has_multiple_mines = yes has_country_flag = riches_found_selection_closed NOT = { riches_found_country_has_selected_mine = yes } }
	}
	available = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
			NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
		}
		OR = { riches_found_country_has_multiple_mines = yes has_country_flag = riches_found_selection_closed NOT = { riches_found_country_has_selected_mine = yes } }
	}
	target_root_trigger = { is_ai = no riches_found_country_has_category_mine = yes }
	target_trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			riches_found_mine_is_registered = yes
			NOT = { riches_found_mine_is_terminal = yes }
		}
	}
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = decision_riches_found_select_mine_tt
		clr_country_flag = riches_found_selection_closed
		set_variable = { riches_found_selected_mine = FROM }
		FROM = { set_state_flag = riches_found_selected_target }
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_value.zero
	}
}

mission_riches_found_secure_rush = {
	icon = GFX_mission_riches_found_secure_rush
	activation = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	available = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
			has_state_flag = riches_found_camp_administration
			has_state_flag = riches_found_claims_resolved
			check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_floor compare = greater_than }
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = var:riches_found_secure_rush_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_secure_rush_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_revenue_legitimacy = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_success
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
					has_state_flag = riches_found_camp_administration
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_secure_rush_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_2 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_partial
					set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
				}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_secure_rush_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_12 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_failure
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_claims_court_open
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_secure_rush_progress } }
	modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }
	}
}

mission_riches_found_open_railhead = {
	icon = GFX_mission_riches_found_open_railhead
	activation = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	available = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
			riches_found_state_has_route_capacity = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_damaged }
			check_variable = { var = riches_found_mine_development value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.development_ready compare = greater_than }
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = var:riches_found_open_railhead_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_open_railhead_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
				add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_4 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_success
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
					OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route has_state_flag = riches_found_port_route }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_open_railhead_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_3 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_6 }
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_partial
					set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
				}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_open_railhead_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_failure
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_open_railhead_progress } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }
	}
	}

mission_riches_found_restore_shaft = {
	icon = GFX_mission_riches_found_restore_shaft
	activation = {
		any_controlled_state = {
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
			has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
		}
	}
	available = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = mission_riches_found_restore_shaft_ready_tt
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_progress
				riches_found_restore_shaft_works_ready = yes
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_repair_started
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = var:riches_found_restore_shaft_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_restore_shaft_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
			riches_found_record_achievement_repair_completed = yes
			riches_found_record_achievement_assistance = yes
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_10 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_8 }
			riches_found_complete_shaft_restoration = yes
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_success
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
		clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_repair_started
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
					has_state_flag = riches_found_repair_started
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_restore_shaft_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_collapsed } }
						add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_8 }
					}
					riches_found_partially_restore_shaft = yes
					set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_partial
					set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
					set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
					clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_repair_started
				}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_restore_shaft_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_12 }
					add_to_variable = { riches_found_extraction_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_15 }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_failure
			set_temp_variable = { riches_found_mission_cooldown_days = constant:riches_found_duration.mission_cooldown_days }
			set_state_flag = { flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_cooldown days = riches_found_mission_cooldown_days }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_repair_started
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_restore_shaft_progress } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { check_variable = { var = riches_found_local_order value = constant:riches_found_decision_threshold.order_low compare = less_than } }
	}
}

			has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
			OR = { has_state_flag = riches_found_rail_route has_state_flag = riches_found_port_route }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_preparation_pending }
			check_variable = {
				var = global.date
				value = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
				has_state_flag = riches_found_mine_state
			}
		}
	}
	cancel_effect = {
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
			clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
			set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_last_shift_invalidated
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	days_mission_timeout = constant:riches_found_duration.evacuation_mission_days
	selectable_mission = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_good = yes
	complete_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce_complete_tt
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
			set_state_flag = riches_found_demons_district_evacuated
			set_state_flag = riches_found_workforce_evacuated
			set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_evacuation_route_valid
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
			set_variable = { riches_found_last_shift_controller = CONTROLLER }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_supernatural_pressure = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_20 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_local_order = constant:riches_found_action_delta.positive_5 }
			add_to_variable = { riches_found_mine_development = constant:riches_found_action_delta.negative_15 }
			riches_found_complete_mission = yes
			riches_found_record_achievement_evacuation = yes
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
			clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
			clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	timeout_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
					has_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce_partial_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
				set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_partial
				set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_last_shift_invalidated
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
				clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = mission_riches_found_evacuate_workforce_failure_tt
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject }
				set_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_failure
				set_state_flag = riches_found_achievement_last_shift_invalidated
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_subject
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_mission_evacuate_workforce_progress
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_authorized
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_ready
				clr_state_flag = riches_found_demons_evacuation_route_active
				clear_variable = riches_found_demons_evacuation_completion_date
			}
		}
		riches_found_reconcile_controller = yes
		riches_found_refresh_mine_state = yes
		riches_found_recount_active_mission_slots = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.medium
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.urgent_factor has_war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_supernatural_crisis } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor FROM = { has_state_flag = riches_found_severe_collapse } }
		modifier = { factor = constant:riches_found_decision_ai.high_factor has_government = democratic }
	}
}

}
